# 539: Chapter 541: A Terrifyingly Real Illusion!

"Hisuian Zoroark? That's pretty interesting."

Blue had also heard the Pokédex's explanation. After a brief pause, he looked intrigued.

Another Pokémon from the Hisui region. By now, Blue had more or less figured it out—there didn't seem to be a single Hisuian Pokémon that was weak.

The earlier Hisuian Samurott and Sneasler had already proven that.

Not only did they have unique signature moves, their overall strength was also extremely high. It was honestly enviable.

Come to think of it…

Blue frowned thoughtfully. The Hisui region was the ancient name for Sinnoh. If the reason Hisuian Pokémon vanished in modern times was due to environmental changes—

Then if a place's environment were restored to what it was like in the Hisui era, would Pokémon unique to that era appear again?

Blue had actually considered letting his main Pokémon breed with Dylan's Hisuian-form Pokémon. But perhaps because of environmental factors,

the eggs that hatched might produce modern forms rather than Hisuian forms.

After all, many regional forms exist precisely because of environmental differences—like Kantonian Vulpix versus Alolan Vulpix, which came about due to the differing climates of Kanto and Alola.

Of course, this was just a thought. The Hisuian environment might simply be impossible to recreate in the modern era.

"Zoroark itself is already a very special Pokémon. I wonder how well Dylan can use this Hisuian Zoroark."

Giovanni was also very interested.

Zoroark—a uniquely special Pokémon from Unova. Why was it considered special?

Because of its ability: Illusion.

Zoroark's raw combat power was actually quite decent. It was a high-speed mixed attacker. You could say it was strong—and you wouldn't be wrong—but you could also say it was only average, and that wouldn't be wrong either.

It was often described as a fast attacker, but in truth, Zoroark's speed didn't quite reach the level of truly "fast" Pokémon.

Compared to genuine speed demons, Zoroark wasn't especially quick. But its Illusion ability was its real core.

Professor Oak once studied Zoroark extensively and gave the following evaluation of its illusionary powers:

"Zoroark can project scenery or scenes stored in its memory directly into another person's brain using powerful psychic waves, allowing them to see illusions. Many travelers have lost their way on their journeys because of these illusions."

Yes—similar to being led astray by ghostly tricks, though with subtle differences.

Zoroark's ability was the creation of illusions—deception targeting the brain and vision. It was somewhat similar to Latias's light refraction.

It couldn't turn illusions into physical reality, but it could perfectly deceive the senses and confuse opponents through sight.

According to Professor Oak's further research, Zoroark's illusionary ability could be greatly enhanced through training, making the illusions far more "real."

So far, the strongest known Zoroark was capable of making hundreds of people see illusions simultaneously.

Hisuian Zoroark, like regular Zoroark, also possessed the Illusion ability. As a true master of illusions among Pokémon,

how outstanding a Zoroark was depended entirely on how well its Illusion ability had been developed.

Giovanni was very much looking forward to seeing how Dylan's Hisuian Zoroark would perform.

On the battlefield.

"If it's Gym Leader Brother…"

Selene narrowed her eyes slightly as she studied the sinister-looking "Arbok."

She wasn't unfamiliar with Arbok—after all, she hadn't spent all that time at the Rota Gym for nothing.

But something felt off. That Arbok's gaze…

Why did it look so gloomy and eerie?

"Lure it in, then test it."

Selene quickly came up with a rough plan.

Yet she felt as if she'd forgotten something important.

"Does it feel like something's not quite right?"

Dylan seemed to guess her thoughts and asked with a smile.

"?"

Selene returned a puzzled look.

Wait—this Arbok didn't have Intimidate?

She suddenly realized it. She remembered clearly—Dylan's Arbok definitely had the Intimidate ability.

So why hadn't it activated?

"Arbok, Dig."

The smile at Dylan's lips deepened as he gave the command.

"Arbok" lowered its head and drilled into the ground like a bolt of lightning, leaving behind only a dark hole.

"Dig? Then I'll force it out."

Selene felt uneasy, but she immediately gave her own command.

"Gengar!"

Mega Gengar floated upward. It didn't have many perfect answers to Dig—but that didn't matter.

Mega Gengar could float!

"Nasty Plot!"

A dark gleam flashed in Mega Gengar's eyes.

At that moment, the ground beneath Mega Gengar cracked open. A "snake's head" burst out from below, instantly drawing Selene's and Mega Gengar's attention.

"Bitter Malice."

A curve formed at Dylan's lips.

Bitter Malice?

Selene froze.

And at that instant, a bizarre surge of dark blue-black energy erupted behind Mega Gengar.

"Gengar!!"

Selene's pupils shrank as she shouted a warning.

Mega Gengar sensed the danger too and tried to stop its setup and dodge—but it was too rushed.

BOOM!

Terrifying resentment-laced energy exploded across Mega Gengar's body. The bone-chilling malice made it scream in pain as it was blasted backward.

Hisuian Zoroark's signature Ghost-type move—Bitter Malice!

Ghost beats Ghost, and this Bitter Malice dealt heavy damage to Mega Gengar.

"This…!"

Selene was stunned. She looked toward the source of the attack—

But in her vision, there was nothing there at all.

Yet she already understood.

"It's Zoroark, isn't it?!"

Selene gritted her teeth.

"Bingo. Correct~ Your reaction was faster than I expected, Selene."

Dylan replied with a smile.

The air rippled.

Amid gasps of shock, a pale, resentful fox stood upright on two legs. Its long white hair writhed like something alive, whipping wildly, while icy golden eyes filled with hatred stared quietly at Mega Gengar.

Hisuian Zoroark!

And the supposed "hole" and "Arbok" vanished completely. The ground was flat and intact, showing no sign of damage.

Clearly, the earlier Dig and Arbok had all been illusions.

Many people swallowed nervously, staring at the terrifying fox. Just its appearance alone sent chills down the spine.

"Hisuian Zoroark—Normal/Ghost type. Selene, this is its first appearance in this world."

Dylan spoke to her calmly.

"Hisuian Zoroark…"

Selene scrunched up her face. No wonder it used that move—what was it called, Bitter Malice.

She'd guessed it might be Zoroark, but she hadn't expected a Hisuian one.

This was bad.

Normal + Ghost was an awful matchup. How was Mega Gengar supposed to fight that?

Normal was immune to Ghost! Good thing she hadn't opened with Curse—and good thing she'd been wary of Dylan from the start.

"Gengar, Dark Pulse!"

After a moment's thought, Selene decided to probe Hisuian Zoroark's strength with Mega Gengar.

Mega Gengar floated up, dark energy gathering in front of it before firing off waves of black pulses toward Zoroark.

A Dark-type move—Dark Pulse!

"Zoroark."

Dylan chuckled softly.

Zoroark raised its head slightly. Its cold golden eyes, unobscured by white hair, glimmered with eerie light.

Darkness fell.

The entire sky abruptly plunged into pitch-black night.

Gasps and screams of fear echoed all around.

Selene stared upward in shock.

The stadium—was gone.

In her vision, everything had changed completely.

Strange, grotesque dead trees stood all around. In the dark, sinister environment, they looked like sentinels—or vengeful spirits.

Fox-shaped shadows with blood-red eyes crouched beneath the trees, watching her through the trunks. Sharp, resentment-filled cries echoed endlessly in her ears, amplifying the eeriness.

Cold.

Bone-piercing cold.

The deep chill made Selene huddle instinctively, her scalp tingling.

What… is this?

What the hell is this?!

She was completely stunned. One moment she'd been in Indigo Stadium—where the hell had she been sent now?!

Selene knew this was probably caused by Hisuian Zoroark's illusion, but damn it—

This is an illusion?!

Isn't this way too exaggerated?!

And it wasn't just her. It seemed like the spectators in the stadium were affected too—the illusion had engulfed everyone.

Just what kind of illusionary power was this?

Mega Gengar also looked around warily at the gloomy surroundings. As a Ghost-type Pokémon that loved pranks, it wasn't exactly afraid of this kind of atmosphere.

But what it couldn't understand was—

Hadn't it been on the battlefield just a moment ago?

And then…

Mega Gengar felt the sinister gazes crawling over its body. It turned to the left rear.

Behind a barren dead tree, a pale, resentful fox with blood-red eyes noticed Mega Gengar's gaze—and suddenly lunged at it.

"Gah—?!"

Mega Gengar panicked and instinctively fired a Shadow Ball.

The fox was blown apart.

But before Mega Gengar could calm down, more and more eerie foxes emerged from behind the dead trees.

Their hatred-filled eyes, bathed in cold moonlight, made Mega Gengar feel true scalp-numbing dread for the first time.

"Ghh… ghh…"

As Selene's mainstay Pokémon, Mega Gengar stared at the dense swarm of pale foxes completely surrounding it. It swallowed nervously and stepped back.

What are these things?!

Did I stumble into a fox den or something?!

And where did my Trainer go?!

The next moment, all the foxes surged forward, pouncing on the isolated Mega Gengar.

Mega Gengar braced itself and began forming Shadow Balls one after another.

But in reality—

"Looks like this illusion is extremely realistic."

Dylan watched the dramatic scene with interest.

On the battlefield, Mega Gengar appeared completely deranged, blasting wildly at empty air as if it had lost its mind. Its cries gradually shifted from panic to madness.

Meanwhile, Hisuian Zoroark stood quietly at a distance, ensuring it wouldn't be hit by Mega Gengar's attacks. Its left eye glowed with eerie light, and its wild white hair writhed like a living thing.

This—

was the power of illusion.

---

---

---

# 540: Chapter 542: You Still Need Hisuian Pokémon

"This is… an illusion?"

Looking at the gloomy, eerie environment on the field, Wallace broke out in a cold sweat.

He didn't know if it was just his imagination, but he even felt a dense chill creeping over him, raising goosebumps all over his body.

"I can't believe it… this is simply unbelievable!"

Steven was equally shocked. This level of illusionary power could practically be called sorcery.

To completely envelop such a massive battlefield in an illusion—and even affect everyone in the entire stadium—could Zoroark really reach this level?

"Terrifying. N, could your Zoroark do something like this?"

Off to the side, Rosa's eyes were wide as saucers. Perhaps because she was a girl, the dark and sinister environment filled her with a bit of fear.

"You're overestimating me."

N gave a bitter smile and shook his head. His Zoroark was also one of his core Pokémon, among the strongest on his team.

But this kind of illusion…

His Zoroark absolutely couldn't pull it off.

"My Zoroark might be able to affect a few thousand people with an illusion, but it wouldn't be this realistic. And… this Hisuian Zoroark's illusion is different."

N lowered his voice.

As a Zoroark trainer, he was keenly aware of the most critical difference.

"What's different?" Rosa and the others were taken aback.

"Zoroark's illusions are purely visual deception. No matter what it turns into or what it shows you, it's fake at its core."

N explained in detail.

"For example, a Zoroark with especially strong illusion abilities can make you see daytime at night, night during the day, or snow in the middle of summer."

"But no matter what you see, it's only visual deception. It doesn't actually create those substances."

Rosa and the others nodded. Even if they hadn't raised Zoroark themselves, they knew this much.

At its core, it was still just an illusion. It was impossible for Zoroark to use illusion imagery to physically affect an opponent.

That was why most Zoroark battles relied on the Illusion ability to deceive an opponent's vision and fight that way.

"But this Hisuian Zoroark is different," N said gravely.

"It created this gloomy forest scene and tricked our vision into seeing nighttime. Logically, this should only affect our sight, but…"

"We actually felt cold?"

Alder immediately grasped what N meant, his expression tightening.

"That's right. We genuinely felt a deep, biting cold."

N frowned. This shouldn't be happening. The weather was great today, with bright sunshine.

Even though visually they couldn't see the sun and only saw a dark sky, that was just the illusion affecting their vision.

In reality, the sun was still shining over the Indigo Plateau. They shouldn't be feeling cold.

"Could it be psychological?" Rosa tilted her head.

"No. Absolutely not."

N shook his head. His mental state was steady—he wasn't affected by the 'ominous forest' or those vengeful foxes. He was simply amazed by how well-developed this Hisuian Zoroark's illusions were.

But that deep chill had made even the hairs on his arms stand on end.

That was real, physical cold.

It shouldn't have been possible. Was there a fundamental difference between Hisuian Zoroark's illusions and normal Zoroark's?

And N's guess was actually correct.

There was a fundamental difference.

A normal Zoroark's illusion was purely illusion, but Hisuian Zoroark's illusion could truly cause damage.

That was because the long fur covering Hisuian Zoroark released resentment-filled energy. It didn't just show terrifying phantoms—it inflicted harm on the opponent both mentally and physically.

Why?

Because Hisuian Zorua itself was born from deep resentment.

Hisuian Zorua came into existence when the powerful hatred of Zorua that died during the Hisui era—toward both humans and Pokémon—was transformed into strength, eventually reincarnating into this form.

As a result, every Hisuian Zorua and Hisuian Zoroark carried intense resentment energy, and that deep hatred allowed their illusions to possess tangible destructive power.

This was the fundamental difference between Hisuian Zoroark's illusions and those of ordinary Zoroark.

On the field.

"Gengar! Calm down! They're all fake!"

Selene shouted urgently at Mega Gengar, which was wildly attacking the vengeful foxes around it.

She was anxious. She knew these were illusions, but Mega Gengar was already being affected. If this continued, it would sink deeper and deeper into the illusion.

Mega Gengar heard Selene's voice. Trusting its Trainer, it forced itself to calm down—but when faced with the terrifying foxes baring their fangs, it still instinctively wanted to fight back.

It held itself back… but—

"Gah?!"

A vengeful fox tore into Mega Gengar's body, ripping open a wound. Mega Gengar groaned in pain and immediately formed a Shadow Ball, blasting the fox to pieces.

"How is that possible?"

Selene stared in disbelief.

Weren't these terrifying foxes just illusions? That had to be the case—so how could they actually injure Mega Gengar?

"Bitter Malice."

As Mega Gengar struggled to deal with the foxes, a dense surge of dark blue energy slammed heavily into its back.

The violent explosion of energy made Mega Gengar scream in pain as its entire body was blasted away.

The next second, it was as if the world had changed.

The dark forest vanished. All the vengeful foxes disappeared. Sunlight poured down once more.

Mega Gengar bared its teeth in pain. Behind it, the pale Hisuian Zoroark hunched slightly, its shadowy gaze coldly fixed on Mega Gengar like a silent wraith.

Seeing that eerie white fox, Selene gritted her teeth and swiftly took out a Poké Ball, recalling Mega Gengar.

This was the best option—to prevent Hisuian Zoroark from continuing its assault.

Besides, Mega Gengar's mental state had clearly been affected. Letting it rest was the right call.

As the sunlight returned, the entire stadium erupted.

"We're back? What just happened?"

"I thought I got isekai'd or something—those foxes were terrifying!"

"So it wasn't just me? That was Zoroark's illusion? How the hell did it do that?!"

"That illusion was way too realistic—damn, I nearly got scared to death!"

"This immersive illusion… guys, I've got a bold idea! Do you think it could make me a Water Dragon Pleasure Park or something?"

The crowd exploded with discussion, many people swearing as they vented their emotions.

Some of them had known about Zoroark's abilities before, but after experiencing it firsthand, they finally understood how people who got trapped by wild Zoroark illusions in the wilderness must have felt.

It was absolutely terrifying.

"An unbelievable illusion… so strong."

Cynthia's beautiful eyes shone with amazement. This kind of illusion was simply unparalleled.

On the field.

"As expected of Gym Leader Brother's Zoroark."

Selene's expression was grave. That brief exchange had already revealed a great deal.

Damn it. She would rather fight something like Articuno than face a Pokémon with abilities like this.

With illusions on this level, defeating Hisuian Zoroark through normal means was just too difficult.

"So how are you planning to deal with it?"

Dylan asked with a gentle smile.

Selene thought it over. Hisuian Zoroark's combat power wasn't weak, but the real threat was that near-divine illusion.

Illusions deceived vision. Even though Hisuian Zoroark seemed to be standing right there, who could say whether that was its real body?

For all she knew, the Zoroark she was seeing was just a fake projection created by the illusion.

An opponent whose true body couldn't even be confirmed was the most troublesome kind.

If only she had Lucario—Aura would let her pinpoint the real Zoroark.

But not being able to find the real body didn't mean there was no way to respond.

Keep it simple.

If you can't find it, don't bother looking.

Just carpet-bomb the field with AoE moves!

"Enamorus!"

Selene didn't hesitate as she threw her second Poké Ball.

A flash of white light, and a pink Pokémon appeared in front of her—one of the Forces of Nature, Enamorus (Incarnate Forme).

Its body was entirely pink, slender in build, with a cloud forming its lower half. A serpent-like tail extended from the cloud, coiling around its body.

"Beep—Enamorus (Incarnate Forme), Fairy/Flying type, the Love-Hate Pokémon.

Ability: Cute Charm

Moves: Springtide Storm, Outrage, Moonblast, Healing Wish, Mystical Fire…

Description: When it flies over the sea, it signals the end of the harsh winter. Legends say its kindness allows new life to sprout across the land of Hisui. (Incarnate Forme)"

"Enamorus!"

Selene raised her Poké Ball and recalled it immediately, as the Dynamax Band on her wrist lit up with a red glow.

"Oh?"

Dylan raised an eyebrow, roughly understanding her plan.

"Decisive. Nicely done."

He chuckled, praising Selene's decisiveness.

Indeed, using Dynamax against Hisuian Zoroark was the best choice. Dynamax moves covered massive areas—if used deliberately, they could blanket the entire battlefield.

That would leave Hisuian Zoroark nowhere to hide.

It was the optimal solution. If Selene stubbornly insisted on fighting normally, Dylan wouldn't have minded letting her experience just how terrifying "illusion" could truly be.

"But this isn't a Gym battle."

Dylan also recalled Hisuian Zoroark. This wasn't a Gym match—he had every right to switch freely.

"If we're fighting Enamorus, then using Hisuian Pokémon is still the most fitting choice."

He took out another Ultra Ball, the corner of his mouth lifting.

"Goodra—prepare for battle!"

---

---

---

# 541: Chapter 543: The Noble Steel Dragon

"Goodra, prepare for battle!"

Dylan flicked his wrist and threw out the second Poké Ball in his hand.

A flash of white light burst forth, and a pale purple Pokémon with a somewhat gloomy expression landed in front of Dylan.

"Huh?"

Selene was just about to throw out her Dynamax Poké Ball, but when she saw this Pokémon, she froze for a moment.

Its appearance was extremely similar to Goodra—almost identical, in fact—except its body looked a bit slimmer than a normal Goodra's.

But the biggest difference was the massive shell on its back, something that looked like a giant snail shell.

Wait—since when did Goodra have a shell on its back?!

Selene was stunned, but she quickly came back to her senses. This was probably another regional form of Goodra, one she'd never heard of before—likely from Hisui again.

"Beep—Goodra (Hisuian Form), Steel/Dragon type, the Shell Dwelling Pokémon.

Ability: Sap Sipper

Moves: Shelter, Heavy Slam, Iron Tail, Muddy Water, Body Slam…

Description: It freely controls a metal shell that can be both hard and soft. It is stubborn by nature and hates being alone; if someone it cares for leaves its side, it flies into a furious rage. (Hisuian Form)"

"Good grief—a Steel/Dragon-type Goodra?"

When Diantha saw this Goodra, her whole demeanor changed, her eyes sparkling.

She herself had a Goodra as one of her main Pokémon, but hers was purely Dragon-type. The one on the field, however, possessed the noble Steel/Dragon typing!

Among pseudo-legendaries, Goodra had always been considered relatively weak. The reason was simple: although its special defense was very high, its pure Dragon typing didn't offer much of an advantage—or disadvantage—compared to other Dragon-type pseudo-legendaries.

Compared to other pseudo-legendary Dragons, it was just… average. Or rather, because its physical defense was relatively low, it was actually on the weaker side.

But Steel plus Dragon?

That was a completely different story!

Even if its stats were unchanged, that typing alone was enough to send Hisuian Goodra straight into god-tier status.

Immune to Poison, resistant to nine different types, and only weak to Fighting and Ground.

Absolutely aristocratic.

"This Hisuian Goodra must be insanely strong," Serena couldn't help but marvel.

If only the Kalos-region Goodra had this typing too…

So enviable.

That said, objectively speaking, that snail shell on Hisuian Goodra's back was really… kind of abstract.

On the field.

Selene had already sent out Enamorus again. A black vortex spun in the sky as Dynamaxed Enamorus descended.

Dylan also recalled Hisuian Goodra, Dynamaxed it, and threw it back out. Bathed in faint red light, the Dynamaxed Hisuian Goodra stood tall before Dynamaxed Enamorus.

The two colossal Pokémon faced each other.

Even though its opponent was the legendary Harbinger of Spring from Hisui, Hisuian Goodra showed no reverence at all.

It didn't care whether the opponent was a god or not.

Anyone who opposed its Trainer would be crushed.

Unlike its "frail" counterparts, Hisuian Goodra was built to fight.

"Enamorus! Max Quake!"

Selene gave the command without hesitation.

"Max Ooze!"

Dylan responded just as swiftly.

Both Dynamaxed Pokémon let out deep roars. Enamorus swung its arm as the ground began to tremble violently.

At the same time, Hisuian Goodra stomped heavily, deep purple toxic sludge surging forward and nearly flooding the entire battlefield.

Boom—!

The ground shook like an earthquake as dense, earthen-yellow energy flared beneath Hisuian Goodra's feet.

An overwhelming burst of ground-type power completely engulfed it.

On Enamorus's side, thick columns of poisonous liquid shot upward, all blasting toward Enamorus.

The deafening explosions overlapped as Max Quake and Max Ooze struck at the same time.

Both Pokémon let out pained cries—after all, these were super-effective attacks, and they hurt.

As Max Ooze and Max Quake landed, Hisuian Goodra's Special Attack rose, while Enamorus's Special Defense increased.

"As expected—its special defense is really high."

Seeing this, Selene felt confirmed in her judgment. Enamorus's Special Attack was extremely strong, yet Hisuian Goodra took a Max Quake head-on without showing much reaction.

Just like normal Goodra, Hisuian Goodra clearly had excellent special bulk.

Still, Enamorus could just barely handle it.

Selene had originally thought Hisuian Goodra would use a Steel-type Max Steelspike instead.

After all, Steel-type Max Steelspike would also be super effective against Enamorus—and it would get STAB too.

But perhaps Dylan thought the Defense-boosting effect of Max Steelspike wasn't very useful.

After all, while Enamorus's physical attack wasn't low, its Special Attack was clearly the stronger stat.

"Keep using Max Quake!"

Selene didn't hesitate and ordered Enamorus to continue.

Although Incarnate Enamorus didn't have particularly high Special Defense, its legendary physique—combined with the fact that Hisuian Goodra's Special Attack wasn't overwhelmingly strong—meant it could endure!

"Max Ooze!"

Dylan showed no sign of backing down.

The second clash of Max Quake and Max Ooze slammed into each other again, thunderous booms echoing across the arena.

"Neither side is willing to give an inch."

Hop couldn't help but click his tongue. Still, this was the most common scenario in Dynamax versus Dynamax battles.

You've already Dynamaxed—why would you back off now?

Otherwise, what was the point of Dynamaxing in the first place?

Except in rare cases, Dynamax battles were simple slugfests.

Whoever fell first lost.

After the second exchange came the third.

Max Quake and Max Ooze struck each other once more. After taking three hits in a row, both Hisuian Goodra and Enamorus could clearly feel their condition deteriorating.

As the third Dynamax moves ended, the Dynamax duration expired.

Both Pokémon rapidly shrank back to their normal sizes, and the black vortexes faded away.

"One side boosted Special Attack, the other boosted Special Defense. It's hard to say who came out ahead."

Hop looked at Hisuian Goodra and Enamorus now facing each other at normal size.

"Under normal circumstances, boosting Special Attack is better," Leon analyzed.

"The attacker controls the tempo. And Enamorus doesn't look like a defensive Pokémon to begin with."

"But Enamorus has the advantage in speed and flight," Gloria added.

"It's hard for Hisuian Goodra to land attacks on it."

"If you can't hit the target, no matter how strong the attack is, it's meaningless."

"Exactly."

Leon nodded in agreement.

On the field.

"Enamorus, Springtide Storm!"

Selene launched another attack without hesitation. She wanted to test Hisuian Goodra's offensive power at this point.

Enamorus cried out sharply as pink light flared across its body. The serpent coiled around it shot its head forward, forming a heart-shaped crest.

A pink Springtide Storm swept through the sky, surging toward Hisuian Goodra below.

This Fairy-type move—Springtide Storm—was Enamorus's signature attack, a devastating gale woven from love and hatred alike.

"Thunder!"

Electricity crackled around Hisuian Goodra as a massive bolt of lightning tore through the air to meet the Springtide Storm.

Boom—!

Thunder and Springtide Storm collided midair, lightning and raging winds clashing violently before exploding apart.

Powerful shockwaves rippled outward, the air itself trembling.

"It blocked it?!"

As fierce winds howled, Selene pressed down on her straw hat and narrowed her eyes at Hisuian Goodra.

Springtide Storm had actually been stopped.

That meant Hisuian Goodra's Special Attack had already been quite high to begin with.

And now, after being boosted three times, its special offense had reached a level comparable to Enamorus's.

That was definitely not good news.

Even though Enamorus's Special Defense was also high now—thanks to three boosts—Selene wasn't about to let it just stand there and tank hits.

"Goodra, Shelter."

While Selene was still thinking, Dylan didn't pause for a second.

Hisuian Goodra withdrew entirely into its shell, its body glowing with intense metallic light.

Shelter—a Steel-type move!

"Earth Power!"

Selene immediately counterattacked.

She didn't know exactly what Shelter did, but there was no way she was just going to let her opponent set up freely.

Enamorus cried out as earthen-yellow energy flared beneath Hisuian Goodra—Ground-type Earth Power.

Boom!

Earth Power erupted violently, torrents of ground energy slamming directly into Hisuian Goodra's shell.

When the attack subsided, Hisuian Goodra emerged from its shell once more.

Shelter complete—its Defense had risen by two stages.

"What a shame. Not the version that boosts evasion."

Dylan thought regretfully.

When Legends: Arceus first released, Hisuian Goodra had once been hailed as the strongest pseudo-legendary.

Why?

Because of its Steel/Dragon typing—and because of Shelter.

In Legends: Arceus, Shelter didn't just boost defense; it also made Hisuian Goodra much harder to hit.

That evasion boost was the real reason it was once considered absurdly overpowered.

But by the Scarlet/Violet era, Shelter had been nerfed hard—essentially becoming a second Iron Defense, boosting Defense by two stages only.

Still, even so, with its excellent typing, Hisuian Goodra remained extremely formidable.

Dylan found it a pity, but it made sense.

Reality wasn't a game—numbers couldn't simply translate directly.

If Shelter boosted evasion in the real world, defining "evasion" itself would be incredibly vague.

"Life Dew."

Dylan issued another command.

After tanking three Max Quakes and one Earth Power, Hisuian Goodra's condition really wasn't great.

But that was fine—it had recovery.

Hisuian Goodra raised its head and sprayed a large amount of water into the air, which scattered and fell as shimmering droplets.

"Earth Power!" Selene ordered immediately.

"Dodge," Dylan said calmly.

Hisuian Goodra wasn't fast, but dodging a single Earth Power wasn't a problem.

It quickly moved out of range as Earth Power erupted uselessly into empty ground.

Meanwhile, the falling Life Dew sprinkled over Hisuian Goodra, restoring its stamina.

Life Dew's recovery wasn't as strong as Recover, but its advantage was flexibility.

Recover required staying still—meaning guaranteed damage from the opponent.

Life Dew didn't.

"This won't work."

Selene frowned slightly.

Boosting defenses and healing—was he worried she'd have Enamorus move in close and use Fighting-type moves?

To be honest, she had been thinking about that.

After all, Enamorus's physical attack was also quite good.

But now… was close combat still the right call?

---

---

---

# 542: Chapter 544: Holy Crap, Selene!

"Come back, Enamorus!"

After a moment's thought, Selene recalled Enamorus.

"Iron Valiant! I'm counting on you!"

Without the slightest hesitation, Selene threw her third Poké Ball.

The future Pokémon Iron Valiant—gripping its gleaming energy blade—landed in front of Selene, coldly staring down Hisuian Goodra.

Fairy- and Fighting-type Iron Valiant!

"Selene is seriously decisive—no hesitation at all."

Seeing Selene switch straight into Iron Valiant, Nemona couldn't help but exclaim in amazement.

This girl was far too decisive.

Although she also believed that it was already difficult for Enamorus to defeat Hisuian Goodra, Enamorus had boosted its Special Defense three times with Max Quake.

Pulling off that kind of triple boost in a normal battle wasn't easy, so most people would've been reluctant to switch it out.

But Selene didn't hesitate in the slightest. The moment she judged the situation to be unfavorable, she acted immediately.

"But her choice was the right one," Qing said quietly.

"If Hisuian Goodra is allowed to keep boosting like this, it'll become truly hard to deal with. Enamorus simply doesn't have enough tools to defeat it quickly."

At this point, Hisuian Goodra was sitting at +3 Special Attack and +2 Defense, and it still had Life Dew for recovery.

Coupled with its already excellent Special Defense, it was extremely difficult for Enamorus to bring it down in a short time.

Once Hisuian Goodra finished setting up completely, it would be a nightmare to handle.

"Exactly. Now's the best chance to take it down. Iron Valiant's offensive pressure is perfect for this," Geeta nodded.

Of course, they were spectators—clear-headed observers.

Geeta knew that if they were actually in Selene's position, they might not have been able to make such a decisive call either.

That was why Nemona was so impressed by Selene's resolve.

On the field.

"So you've switched to Iron Valiant, huh?"

Dylan examined the Iron Valiant he had personally handed over to Selene in the past, smiling faintly.

Compared to the last time she challenged his gym, this battle-hardened Iron Valiant was clearly much stronger now.

Iron Valiant smoothly shifted its radiant blade from its right hand to its left and slowly raised it, its razor-sharp aura chilling to the bone.

Hisuian Goodra seemed to sense the danger emanating from Iron Valiant and let out a low growl.

"Come on."

Dylan raised his arm and gestured invitingly at Selene and Iron Valiant.

Let me see how strong you've become.

"Iron Valiant!"

Selene curled her lips into a sharp grin, her eyes blazing.

"Charge in—Aura Sphere!"

Iron Valiant's gaze remained cold as it started forward, its steps quickening until it broke into a sprint toward Hisuian Goodra.

A blue Aura Sphere formed at the tip of the blade in its left hand and was fired straight at Hisuian Goodra.

A Fighting-type Aura Sphere!

Hisuian Goodra's Steel/Dragon typing gave it phenomenal resistances—but it was weak to Fighting!

"Flash Cannon!"

Hisuian Goodra charged energy in its mouth, firing a metallic beam head-on at the Aura Sphere.

Boom!

Flash Cannon and Aura Sphere collided violently—but it was the Aura Sphere that was forcibly blasted apart.

Against a Hisuian Goodra with +3 Special Attack, Iron Valiant's special offense simply couldn't compete.

The Flash Cannon continued onward toward Iron Valiant.

"Dodge!" Selene shouted.

Iron Valiant slid to the side, moving with fluid precision as it avoided the beam and closed the distance to Hisuian Goodra.

At the same time, it raised its shining blade horizontally, slotting both arms into the weapon as the single blade split into twin arm-blades—making it look even more like Gallade.

The distance between Hisuian Goodra and Iron Valiant rapidly shrank!

The entire stadium's attention locked onto the field.

Close combat was about to begin!

"It's coming! Goodra!" Dylan called out.

Hisuian Goodra growled at the charging Iron Valiant, bracing itself.

"Shelter!"

"Brick Break!"

Hisuian Goodra withdrew fully into its shell once more, metallic light flaring—Steel-type Shelter!

Meanwhile, Iron Valiant's twin arm-blades gleamed white as it accelerated again, slashing straight down at Hisuian Goodra.

A Fighting-type Brick Break!

The strike slammed hard against Hisuian Goodra's shell, sending a jolt up Iron Valiant's arms—it felt like cutting into solid steel.

Much like Blastoise's shell, Hisuian Goodra's shell was incredibly tough—especially while Shelter was active.

Hisuian Goodra emerged again, its Defense rising once more.

But Iron Valiant had been waiting for that exact moment.

Twisting its footing, it swung its right arm-blade through the air, slashing viciously toward Hisuian Goodra.

"Flash Cannon!"

Hisuian Goodra showed no fear, metallic energy surging in its mouth.

It was preparing to trade damage.

After using Shelter twice, Hisuian Goodra's Defense was extremely high—even a super-effective Brick Break was manageable.

But Iron Valiant's Special Defense was low. Trading hits here was clearly advantageous.

"Iron Valiant!"

Selene understood that perfectly.

No way was she allowing a damage trade.

Iron Valiant adjusted the angle of its blade mid-swing and smashed it straight into Hisuian Goodra's face, forcibly knocking its head to the side.

Boom!

The metallic beam blasted out—but straight into the sky.

"Nian!!"

Hisuian Goodra let out a furious roar.

"Iron Valiant! Low Kick!"

Iron Valiant's eyes were icy as it dropped low and swept its leg hard into Hisuian Goodra's footing—a Fighting-type Low Kick!

Bang!

Hisuian Goodra cried out in pain as its massive body tilted and crashed toward the ground.

Seeing this, Selene's grin widened.

The heavier the opponent, the stronger Low Kick became—and Hisuian Goodra was definitely no lightweight.

The damage from that strike was substantial.

"Goodra!" Dylan suddenly called out.

As Hisuian Goodra fell, the shell on its back turned flexible, its tail extending outward.

"Iron Tail!"

In the next instant, Hisuian Goodra's tail gleamed with metallic light and whipped violently toward Iron Valiant.

A classic monster move—smacking the hero with a tail while falling.

Steel-type Iron Tail!

"Iron Valiant!" Selene's pupils shrank.

This was completely unexpected.

Even Iron Valiant couldn't evade it in time.

The Iron Tail struck squarely, sending Iron Valiant flying backward.

Steel-type Iron Tail dealt super-effective damage to Iron Valiant.

"This is bad—that Iron Tail actually landed."

Wallace frowned slightly. Hitting Iron Valiant like that wasn't easy.

"Yeah, that hit must've hurt. Iron Valiant's defenses aren't great—it was clearly prepared in advance," Steven nodded.

Naturally, they were rooting for Selene.

After all, Dylan the final boss—everyone knew how terrifying he was.

"But Low Kick did a lot of damage too. Hisuian Goodra should be in pretty bad shape now," Rosa said, watching Hisuian Goodra crash heavily to the ground.

Its sheer weight made Low Kick especially punishing.

"Just keep it steady—Iron Valiant can win this," Rosa said confidently.

That Iron Tail had been a surprise, but Iron Valiant still held the advantage.

Against a high-defense, counter-focused Pokémon like Hisuian Goodra, a fast dual-attacker like Iron Valiant controlled the pace.

And unlike something absurdly agile like Gliscor, Hisuian Goodra lacked the mobility to consistently catch Iron Valiant.

"Now it comes down to Selene's execution," Steven agreed.

On the field.

"Iron Valiant, are you okay?" Selene asked.

Iron Valiant gave a low cry, indicating it was fine. The Iron Tail had been heavy, but at full health, it could take a hit like that.

Selene let out a breath of relief.

Good thing it was Iron Tail and not Flash Cannon—Iron Valiant's Special Defense was truly awful.

"Iron Valiant, Aura Sphere!"

Selene turned her gaze to the Hisuian Goodra slowly pushing itself up.

Because of that Iron Tail, she'd missed a perfect follow-up opportunity—but it didn't matter.

Hisuian Goodra was already on its last legs.

It couldn't take much more!

Iron Valiant raised its right arm, forming an Aura Sphere and firing it straight at Hisuian Goodra.

"Flash Cannon!"

Hisuian Goodra immediately fired back.

Flash Cannon smashed through Aura Sphere and surged toward Iron Valiant—but Iron Valiant nimbly dodged again, accelerating as it closed in.

"Gyro Ball!"

Hisuian Goodra spun rapidly, transforming into a metallic, spinning sphere.

Steel-type Gyro Ball—its power increased the slower the user was.

Hisuian Goodra was far slower than Iron Valiant, so this Gyro Ball hit hard.

But for Selene, this was actually an opening.

"Close Combat!" she shouted.

Iron Valiant surged forward, limbs glowing white as it unleashed a flurry of punches, slashes, and kicks against the spinning Gyro Ball.

A brutal Fighting-type Close Combat!

Under the relentless assault, Hisuian Goodra's rotation slowed, until Iron Valiant's final strike forcibly brought it to a halt.

"Focus Blast!"

"Thunder!"

Dylan gave the order without hesitation. At this distance, Thunder was far more likely to hit.

Iron Valiant cupped its hands as a Fighting-type Focus Blast formed and slammed directly into Hisuian Goodra's chest.

At the same time, crackling lightning surged over Hisuian Goodra and struck Iron Valiant.

Boom!

Focus Blast and Thunder detonated almost simultaneously.

Smoke billowed from the explosion as Iron Valiant was blasted backward by the Thunder.

Thud!

Iron Valiant hit the ground, rolling twice before barely regaining its footing.

Thunder had done serious damage.

With its low Special Defense—and Hisuian Goodra's massive Special Attack—the hit hurt badly.

Iron Valiant was heavily injured.

But—

As the smoke slowly cleared, Hisuian Goodra's figure came back into view.

"It's still standing?!"

Everyone was stunned.

How was that possible?

Just how much damage had this Hisuian Goodra taken from start to finish?!

Gasps echoed across the stadium.

Selene clenched her teeth, about to order Iron Valiant to attack again.

No matter what, this Hisuian Goodra—already barely hanging on—had to go down.

But then…

"Nian…"

Hisuian Goodra's body finally went limp, collapsing to the ground with a heavy crash.

In the end, it couldn't hold on anymore.

# 543: Chapter 545: Dylan’s Third Pokémon!

"Hisuian Goodra has lost its ability to battle. Iron Valiant wins!"

The referee announced the result.

The moment the decision was made, the entire stadium erupted into thunderous cheers.

"Holy crap—Selene!!"

"Selene is insane! She actually took down one of Champion Dylan's Pokémon first!"

"That was awesome! Iron Valiant is way too cool!"

"Let's go, Demon King's disciple! The burden of defeating the Demon King rests on you!"

"That Hisuian Goodra was absurdly tanky though!"

To be fair, a lot of the crowd were actually Dylan's fans—but that didn't stop them from cheering for his opponent.

After all… Dylan was just too invincible. Seeing a challenger who could really push him was rare and exciting.

"As expected of Selene. She actually scored the first knockout,"

Wallace said happily from the stands, clearly pleased.

"Mm, but Iron Valiant isn't in great shape either,"

Steven observed quietly, watching Iron Valiant.

Although it defeated Hisuian Goodra, the price it paid was huge.

Enamorus was already worn down, and Iron Valiant was the same—having taken both Iron Tail and Thunder, it was now badly injured.

Winning under these conditions couldn't really be called an advantage.

Still, no matter what, forcing Dylan to lose a Pokémon first was worth celebrating.

"Rest well."

Dylan recalled Hisuian Goodra, speaking gently to it.

He knew this wasn't because Hisuian Goodra hadn't tried hard enough.

A heavy, defensive, counter-focused Pokémon like Hisuian Goodra was simply limited by its nature—against Enamorus and Iron Valiant, it just didn't have much room to operate.

"Being able to defeat my Goodra, Selene—you did well,"

Dylan said with a smile as he put away the Poké Ball.

"Hehe~! Of course I did!"

Selene beamed, her small face glowing with pride.

"Then next," Dylan continued,"how do you plan to deal with this one?"

He took out an Ultra Ball and flicked it forward.

"Your next opponent… is this!"

"Arcanine! Prepare for battle!"

With a powerful roar full of vigor, a Pokémon resembling a majestic lion leapt onto the field, landing in front of Dylan and staring down Iron Valiant with imposing presence.

"This is… Arcanine?"

Selene blinked.Another Hisuian one, right?

Its fur was gray-black, its overall silhouette like a massive male lion. Though it retained Arcanine's familiar features, this one looked far more ferocious and imposing than the noble, heroic look of the modern form.

Hisuian Arcanine.

『Ding—Arcanine (Hisuian Form), Fire/Rock type, Legendary PokémonAbility: IntimidateMoves: Raging Fury, Extreme Speed, Fire Blast, Crunch, Morning Sun…Description: It lunges at prey with violently burning flames wrapped around its fangs. Despite its massive size, it uses agile feints to toy with opponents, moving as if it were dancing.』

"Tch—figures it's Hisuian Arcanine too,"

Leaf said enviously as she looked at the fierce yet majestic Pokémon.

This was already the third Hisuian Pokémon Dylan had used in this match.

Seriously—did he go to Hisui to stock up or something?

Leaf was already wondering whether this Hisuian Arcanine was male or female, and whether it could breed with her own Arcanine.

The offspring would definitely be incredible!

"Hisuian Arcanine—Fire and Rock type. Selene, this one's much harder to deal with than Hisuian Goodra,"

Dylan said, giving her a brief warning.

"Fire… Rock?"

Selene's expression turned subtle.

Because that typing was actually very easy to target.

In truth, Hisuian Arcanine's typing was kind of awkward.

Regular Arcanine was pure Fire. While it had fewer resistances, it didn't suffer from Hisuian Arcanine's glaring weaknesses.

Hisuian Arcanine gained more resistances—but also picked up brutal four-times weaknesses to Ground and Water.

And those were extremely common attacking types.

That was where Terastallization came in.

Once it Terastallized, Hisuian Arcanine's typing would change, eliminating those crippling 4× weaknesses and massively boosting its viability.

That's why Terastallization was said to have "saved" so many Pokémon—letting their true power shine.

"Arcanine."

Dylan took out a black Tera Orb, making Selene click her tongue.

As expected—he was going to Terastallize it.

Those two four-times weaknesses were basically an open invitation to be targeted.

Dylan tossed the Tera Orb toward Hisuian Arcanine. Crystal-like light flared, followed by a sharp chime.

A Fire-Tera Hisuian Arcanine now stood proudly on the field.

"Iron Valiant, be careful," Selene said seriously.

Iron Valiant raised its twin arm-blades, fully alert.

"Alright then, Selene,"

Dylan smiled.

Hisuian Arcanine lowered its body, muscles tensed.

An invisible pressure spread out.

A layer of white light dispersed from Iron Valiant's body—Intimidate had lowered its Attack.

"Extreme Speed!"

In the next instant, Hisuian Arcanine shot forward like an arrow, so fast it left only a red afterimage.

Normal-type Extreme Speed!

"Iron Valiant—Quick Guard!"

Selene shouted immediately.

Fighting-type Quick Guard could block priority moves.

Iron Valiant locked onto the blazing red streak, taking a defensive stance.

In a blink—

White light flared in front of it.

Quick Guard!

But—

Whoosh!

The rush of wind brushed past Iron Valiant's face.

There was nothing in front of it.

Iron Valiant's pupils shrank.

Hisuian Arcanine hadn't rammed into it at all—it had slipped right past!

It had been played.

"Iron Valiant! Watch out!" Selene yelled.

The Arcanine that had sprinted past immediately pivoted, charging back in from behind—

Still Extreme Speed!

Iron Valiant barely had time to turn before Hisuian Arcanine slammed hard into its chest.

Bang!

Iron Valiant staggered backward. Already weakened, that hit hurt.

"Fire Blast!"

Using the recoil to leap back, Hisuian Arcanine gathered flames in its mouth.

A blazing, gigantic 大-shaped inferno crashed down.

Fire-type Fire Blast!

"Iron Valiant, Brick Break!" Selene's expression changed.

Iron Valiant steadied itself, crossing its arm-blades in front of its body.

Boom!

Fire Blast smashed directly into Brick Break.

Blazing flames surged violently—Fire-Tera Fire Blast was terrifyingly powerful.

Iron Valiant gritted its teeth, bursting with strength as it hacked forward, forcibly splitting the inferno apart.

The flames exploded outward, blasting Iron Valiant backward.

"Extreme Speed."

Before Iron Valiant could even regain its footing, a red figure burst out of the flames.

Hisuian Arcanine ignored the lingering fire entirely, charging straight through it and slamming into Iron Valiant again.

Iron Valiant let out a pained grunt as it was sent flying.

"Flamethrower!"

Hisuian Arcanine didn't stop.

While sprinting, it leapt and unleashed a roaring pillar of fire downward.

Boom!

Flamethrower hit cleanly.

Flames and shockwaves exploded outward.

Hisuian Arcanine landed almost soundlessly, its Fire-Tera crystal body gleaming red, head held high like a proud, ferocious lion.

The flames dissipated.

Iron Valiant lay unconscious.

"Iron Valiant has lost the ability to battle. Hisuian Arcanine wins!"

The referee raised the flag.

The crowd exploded.

"It's so cool!"

"I still think normal Arcanine looks better, but damn—this one fighting at full sprint is insanely cool."

"It beat Iron Valiant that easily? Is Champion Dylan getting serious now?"

"Compared to the passive Hisuian Goodra, this Hisuian Arcanine's pressure is on a whole different level."

The stadium buzzed with discussion.

"That's rough—this Hisuian Arcanine is strong,"

Nemona said, eyes sparkling with excitement.

She had a special enthusiasm for Pokémon she'd never seen before.

"Mm. Iron Valiant wasn't at full strength, but Hisuian Arcanine's performance was outstanding,"

Geeta analyzed calmly.

"The pressure from Extreme Speed is intense, and its follow-up speed is frightening. This is going to put a lot more pressure on Selene."

Extreme Speed → Fire Blast → Extreme Speed → Flamethrower.

That was all Hisuian Arcanine had used.

Yet the sheer high-speed oppression it displayed was terrifying.

Sure, Iron Valiant was injured—but that didn't diminish Hisuian Arcanine's overwhelming dominance.

"Hisuian Goodra fought defensively. Hisuian Arcanine fights proactively. Of course the pressure's different,"

Blue shook her head.

An attacker and a defender created completely different kinds of stress for their opponent.

"I wonder how Selene plans to deal with this Hisuian Arcanine,"

Nemona rested her chin on her hand, eyes shining.

This kind of battle—

It was just too good to watch.

# 544: Chapter 546: Against Me?

"So vicious…"

Selene recalled Iron Valiant, her fair little face tightening into a frown.

Judging from Hisuian Arcanine's ferocious combo just now, this Pokémon was overwhelmingly aggressive—completely different from Hisuian Goodra or the illusion-based Hisuian Zoroark.

No wonder Dylan had chosen to Terastallize it.

A Pokémon with such overwhelming frontal combat power was extremely hard to deal with.

"Gengar!"

After a brief moment of thought, Selene decisively threw out Mega Gengar's Poké Ball.

"Heh heh heh~"

Accompanied by an eerie chuckle, Mega Gengar rose from the shadows.

After resting for a while, Mega Gengar's mental state had recovered quite a bit.

"As expected—Gengar. A good choice," Dylan said calmly, unsurprised.

The oppressive pressure of Extreme Speed was obvious, and his Hisuian Arcanine clearly excelled at it.

Mega Gengar's Ghost typing made it immune to Normal-type Extreme Speed, and its proficiency with status moves allowed it to avoid direct confrontation.

Using Mega Gengar to counter Hisuian Arcanine was, objectively speaking, a very smart decision.

"Gengar!"

Selene called out.

Mega Gengar spread a wicked grin as dark light flashed in its eyes. A hazy black shadow covered Hisuian Arcanine's silhouette.

With Shadow Tag active, Hisuian Arcanine could no longer be switched out normally.

"Extreme Speed!"

Dylan launched the attack immediately.

Hisuian Arcanine shot forward in an instant, its fiery red form tearing across the battlefield like lightning.

Even though Extreme Speed was ineffective against Mega Gengar, it was still perfect for closing the distance.

"Gengar! Toxic Spikes!"

Selene gave the order at once.

Mega Gengar floated upward, its claws coming together as a deep-purple energy sphere formed.

Dylan let out a soft chuckle.

"Arcanine!"

Hisuian Arcanine dashed beneath Mega Gengar and leapt high into the air.

"Crunch!"

Boosted by Extreme Speed, its movement was terrifyingly fast. It shot past Mega Gengar, the Extreme Speed fading as it twisted midair and turned back down.

Black light flared from its jaws—Dark-type Crunch!

The Toxic Spikes were hurled skyward, but Mega Gengar itself couldn't evade in time.

Crunch!

Hisuian Arcanine bit down hard on Mega Gengar's head, drawing a shrill cry of pain.

It swung Mega Gengar violently before hurling it straight toward the ground.

Mega Gengar slammed down like a cannonball, baring its teeth in agony—its condition now extremely poor.

"Fire Blast!"

Dylan pressed the attack without mercy. This was the perfect moment—Mega Gengar wouldn't even have time to use Destiny Bond.

Deep-purple droplets rained down, sinking into the battlefield and vanishing. A faint violet gleam flickered, then disappeared.

Toxic Spikes were set.

Mid-fall, Hisuian Arcanine's mouth filled with blazing crimson flames.

A massive Fire Blast crashed down, smashing directly into the Mega Gengar that was trying to sink into the ground.

Boom!

Violent flames erupted, heat waves surging skyward—an overwhelmingly powerful Fire Blast.

"Gengar!" Selene's expression changed slightly—but she didn't panic.

The attack had come far too fast. She hadn't expected Hisuian Arcanine's follow-up to be this brutal.

"That fast-paced offense is insane—Mega Gengar might not hold on," Iris muttered.

"That Fire Blast was extremely powerful. If nothing unexpected happens, Mega Gengar won't survive it," N agreed.

Mega Gengar already had low defenses. It had been hit twice by Hisuian Zoroark's retaliatory attacks earlier, then taken Crunch just now.

Three super-effective hits—and still standing. That alone was impressive.

But surviving Fire Blast? Unlikely.

On the field—

As Hisuian Arcanine landed, deep-purple light suddenly flared across its body.

Toxic Spikes damage triggered.

Many spectators wore strange expressions.

Toxic Spikes was something Dylan himself had popularized.

And now—he was the one being poisoned.

What's more, it was his own student who'd done it.

Dylan glanced at Hisuian Arcanine. One layer of Toxic Spikes was completely manageable.

But Mega Gengar—

"Pain Split!"

Selene suddenly shouted.

"Oh?" Dylan raised an eyebrow.

From the shadows nearby, Mega Gengar's faint red eyes lit up as it slowly rose from underground, barely hanging on.

White light flowed from both Pokémon—Normal-type Pain Split.

The energy merged, split evenly, and returned to both.

In an instant, Hisuian Arcanine's aura weakened noticeably, while Mega Gengar's condition visibly improved.

"So you used Endure," Dylan said calmly.

Otherwise, Mega Gengar couldn't possibly have survived that Fire Blast.

"Hehe~ You're sharp as always, Dylan," Selene laughed.

Endure had let Mega Gengar cling to life.

The situation had been too sudden—Destiny Bond wasn't even an option.

But this Endure into Pain Split combo dragged Hisuian Arcanine's condition down hard.

"Now the advantage is mine! Dylan—no one understands how scary Toxic Spikes are better than you do, right?"

Selene smiled sharply.

With Toxic Spikes on the field, a grounded Pokémon like Arcanine was at a natural disadvantage.

Unless Dylan switched.

"You're confident," Dylan said lightly. "Then let me crush that confidence."

Selene tilted her head.

"Toxic Spikes aren't removed only by clearing moves," Dylan replied, eyes on Mega Gengar.

"Arcanine—Dig."

Hisuian Arcanine plunged underground.

"Flare Blitz!"

Dylan continued.

A low, rumbling roar echoed beneath the battlefield.

"Using Flare Blitz underground?" Selene froze—then her eyes widened.

Was he planning to vaporize the Toxic Spikes with extreme heat?

That sounded impossible…

…Except it actually might work.

Heat began seeping up from the ground. The battlefield grew hotter and hotter beneath Selene's sandals.

Red light glowed faintly under the surface. Cracks spread. Flames leaked out.

The air became scorching.

Something was very wrong.

"Gengar! Get ready—Protect!"

Selene ordered instantly.

Mega Gengar floated up, green light gathering beneath it—but it hesitated, staring at the ground.

Then—

BOOM!

Like a volcanic eruption, massive torrents of fire burst from the entire battlefield.

Mega Gengar instantly used Protect.

Towering flames dyed the sky crimson, reflecting in the stunned eyes of every spectator.

Blazing firestorms surged outward. Selene had to shield her face—the wind itself was burning hot.

One second.

Two seconds.

Three full seconds passed before the flames finally subsided.

The arena's temperature had risen dramatically. The battlefield itself was scorched dry, white steam rising from the ground.

Hisuian Arcanine emerged, landing on the blistering earth, head held high—wild, ferocious, and domineering.

And this time—

No Toxic Spikes damage triggered.

Protect faded. Mega Gengar hovered above, staring down at Hisuian Arcanine.

The two Pokémon faced each other once more.

But the stadium had completely exploded.

"WHAT WAS THAT?!"

"The whole field erupted in flames—are you telling me that was Flare Blitz?!"

"That was insane!"

"This is what Fire-type trainers live for!"

"Using Flare Blitz underground like that—learned something new today!"

The crowd was roaring.

"Did you get it?" Dylan said calmly, smiling at Selene's twitching mouth.

"Even without Defog or Rapid Spin, there are other ways to clear Toxic Spikes."

Selene rolled her eyes.

So that worked too.

Her Incineroar could do something similar—but she'd never even thought of it.

Even after training under Dylan and developing unconventional battle instincts, this kind of tactic…

It was ridiculous.

"Seriously, Dylan… If this keeps up, Toxic Spikes are going to lose value again."

"Not necessarily," Dylan said with a chuckle.

This method, inspired by anime-style tactics, wasn't easy to replicate.

Toxic Spikes couldn't be burned away with ordinary flames—it required sustained, extremely high temperatures.

And that demanded raw power.

Stronger Pokémon created stronger moves.

Likewise, stronger Toxic Spikes were harder to erase.

Mega Gengar's Toxic Spikes, for instance—even a normal Fire-type could burn for an hour and still fail to melt them.

In the end, it all came down to strength.

"Let's continue, Selene."

Dylan cut the conversation short.

"Arcanine—Extreme Speed!"

Hisuian Arcanine burst forward again, energy surging.

"Gengar! Stay sharp!"

Mega Gengar hovered tensely, eyes locked on the red blur.

In an instant, Hisuian Arcanine appeared behind it and leapt.

"Now—Shadow Ball!"

Selene swung her arm.

Mega Gengar retreated while forming a Shadow Ball and firing it—

But Hisuian Arcanine arched backward, dodging cleanly, then flipped and charged again.

It was the motion of Aerial Ace—performed mid–Extreme Speed.

Selene stared in disbelief.

That was even possible?!

But there was no time.

Hisuian Arcanine was already in Mega Gengar's face.

"Crunch!"

Its jaws snapped shut again.

Crunch hit squarely, Mega Gengar shrieking as it clawed desperately at Arcanine's face.

But Arcanine didn't let go—shaking it violently before hurling it down once more.

Mega Gengar smashed into the ground, eyes squeezed shut in pain.

"Fire Blast!"

Dylan gave the finishing order.

He glanced at Selene—by now, she'd probably use that.

Hisuian Arcanine unleashed another Fire Blast from above.

"Gengar! Destiny Bond!"

Selene shouted.

This was the second time—she was ready.

If Mega Gengar was going down, it would take Arcanine with it.

Mega Gengar forced itself to look up, eerie light flaring in its eyes.

"Too predictable, Selene," Dylan said with a smile.

Selene's face changed instantly.

The Fire Blast slammed into the ground—not Mega Gengar.

A feint!

The flames still exploded outward, scorching Mega Gengar painfully—but not enough to knock it out.

Destiny Bond expired.

And it couldn't be used again immediately.

Hisuian Arcanine landed, leapt again—

Another Fire Blast fired point-blank, striking Mega Gengar as it tried to sink into the ground.

Boom!

The explosion engulfed the field.

As the smoke cleared, Mega Gengar had reverted to normal Gengar, eyes spinning as it lay at the bottom of a crater.

"Gengar has lost the ability to battle. Hisuian Arcanine wins."

The referee announced.

Selene recalled Gengar, staring at the Poké Ball with a headache.

Using what Dylan taught her—against Dylan himself—

Really didn't work at all.

He saw through everything.

# 545: Chapter 547: Dylan’s Abilities

"Selene's having a rough time now…"

Blue clicked his tongue. Mega Gengar had already lost the ability to battle, and if Selene hadn't used Pain Split midway to drag down Hisuian Arcanine's condition—

That Hisuian Arcanine would probably still be in excellent shape right now.

"That Hisuian Arcanine… its combat power is completely at ace level."

Giovanni narrowed his eyes slightly as he looked at the ferocious, imposing Hisuian Arcanine—especially considering that Terastallization had eliminated its two biggest quadruple weaknesses.

"How many main forces has Dylan trained, anyway?"

Giovanni shook his head helplessly. No matter how he thought about it, Dylan was simply outrageous.

And Pokémon like these Hisuian forms—Dylan had never used them before. They had to be newly trained, right?

Yet their battle strength was already this high.

Could this be Dylan's Viridian Power?

"…It might actually be."

Giovanni glanced at Yellow, who was staring at Dylan with sparkling eyes full of admiration, and seriously considered the possibility.

After all, Yellow already existed—someone who could temporarily boost a Pokémon's strength.

So why wouldn't there be someone like Dylan, whose power accelerated training speed?

That would perfectly explain why Dylan raised Pokémon so fast.

According to a certain unnamed girl, back then two identical Litten were raised side by side.

After just one week, hers was still acting cute—

While Dylan's had already evolved into Incineroar and was out there slaughtering everything in sight.

Was that something normal logic could explain?

Absolutely not.

That had to be Viridian Power cheating the system.

"Why doesn't my Viridian Power do that…?"Even Giovanni couldn't help but feel envious.

On the battlefield

"Weezing!"

After hesitating briefly, Selene finally threw her fourth Poké Ball. Weezing appeared in front of her.

"Weezing, huh? Looks like she doesn't plan on dealing with Hisuian Arcanine head-on."

"The problem is that Arcanine really is too strong. Extreme Speed itself is already unreasonable."

Nemona and Geeta discussed quietly.

Sending out Weezing clearly wasn't meant for a straight fight.

This was a one-for-one exchange.

Weezing knew Destiny Bond.It knew Explosion.

A professional mutual-destruction specialist.

"But Champion Dylan definitely anticipated this," Leaf said calmly."He can tell what Selene's thinking."

Mega Gengar's Destiny Bond had already been "cracked" earlier.

And Explosion…

"It's strong, sure—but if the opponent can use Protect in time, it's very hard to succeed."

Normally, Destiny Bond and Explosion work best when they're unexpected.

Right now, Selene sending out Weezing—anyone could guess what she wanted.

"But from another angle," Geeta suddenly said,"Maybe Selene is trying to force Dylan to hesitate."

Nemona and Leaf froze.

On the battlefield

"Weezing, huh."Dylan smiled faintly.

"Extreme Speed!"

Hisuian Arcanine burst forward at full stride, charging straight at Weezing at terrifying speed.

"Weezing! Sludge Bomb!"

Weezing spat out deep-purple sludge bombs, predicting Arcanine's running path.

But Hisuian Arcanine didn't dodge at all.

It threaded between the sludge bombs at blinding speed, weaving left and right effortlessly.

To the audience, all they saw was a red blur slicing through the projectiles—afterimages trailing behind it.

Gasps and screams erupted.

It was just too cool.

Bang!

Extreme Speed slammed straight into Weezing, sending it tumbling backward.

"Fire Blast!"

Using the rebound from the impact, Hisuian Arcanine charged flames in its mouth while sliding back.

A massive Fire Blast roared out.

"Protect!"

Weezing's physical defense was very high—Extreme Speed hadn't hurt it much.

A green barrier flashed up just in time.

Boom!

Fire Blast exploded violently, flames painting the sky red.

But the attack failed to break Protect.

The flames faded. The barrier disappeared.

Then—

Bang!

Another collision.

Hisuian Arcanine struck again with Extreme Speed, charging through the lingering flames.

This time, it didn't retreat.

"Flamethrower!"

At point-blank range, blazing fire erupted from Arcanine's mouth.

Boom!

The raging column of flame struck Weezing head-on.

There was no time to defend.

The fire slammed it down, smashing it into the ground.

A perfect hit.

"So vicious…!"Selene's delicate face scrunched up again.

She'd lost count of how many times she'd complained like this already.

Hisuian Arcanine's offense was simply too violent.

Not only were the attacks powerful, but the follow-ups were relentless—no breathing room at all.

"Flare Blitz!"

Dylan wasn't finished.

Hisuian Arcanine dove from midair, flames exploding around its body.

Fire-type Flare Blitz!

BOOOOOOM!!

The attack slammed into Weezing, which hadn't even stood up yet.

Explosive flames and shockwaves tore through the battlefield, cracking the ground apart.

The entire arena shook.

"Holy crap! Extreme Speed, Flamethrower, Flare Blitz—how is that so smooth?!"Rosa stared, dumbfounded.

"That Weezing's getting completely wrecked. No way it can withstand an assault like that," Alder exclaimed.

N glanced toward Selene.

The smoke and embers cleared.

Hisuian Arcanine stood proudly at the edge of a massive crater.

Red lightning crackled across its body—the recoil damage from Flare Blitz.

It let out a low grunt.

Flare Blitz hurt, but it was already used to it.

"Toxic!"

Selene's voice rang out sharply.

"…Hmm?"Dylan looked toward the crater.

Weezing—previously motionless—suddenly sprang up.

A splash of dark-purple venom sprayed straight at Hisuian Arcanine.

Arcanine tried to dodge, but the recoil slowed it just enough.

Some venom struck home.

The poison sank in instantly.

Dark purple light flared across Arcanine's body, its expression twisting in pain.

Badly poisoned.

The first tick of Toxic wasn't too painful—but Hisuian Arcanine was furious.

You disgusting mutant ball.

"Extreme Speed!"

Dylan ordered immediately.

The poison damage ended—and Hisuian Arcanine vanished.

"Weezing—!"

Bang!

Extreme Speed smashed into Weezing again, slamming it to the ground.

"Flamethrower!"

Dylan glanced at Selene.What would she choose?

Hisuian Arcanine charged flames again.

"Explosion!"

Blinding white light erupted from Weezing.

For this moment—

I am the sun.

Weezing's expression was utterly deranged.

"Arcanine!"

Hisuian Arcanine retreated instantly, moving at full speed.

BOOOOOOM!!

Weezing detonated completely.

A massive shockwave exploded outward, wind pressure so violent no one could keep their eyes open.

Normal-type Explosion.

As the dust finally settled—

Hisuian Arcanine lay collapsed in a corner of the field, face twisted in pain.

Dark-purple poison light flickered again.

Toxic damage triggered once more.

At the crater's center, Weezing lay unconscious—Explosion guaranteed that.

But the key was Arcanine.

Though it escaped the blast's center, the explosion's massive range still clipped it.

And more importantly—

It was badly poisoned.

That was why Dylan hadn't ordered Protect earlier.

If Toxic triggered mid-guard, it would have been over.

Even now, after the poison tick ended, Hisuian Arcanine was barely standing.

It turned its head toward Dylan.

Dylan nodded gently—no disappointment in his eyes.

Only then did Hisuian Arcanine finally relax.

It still mustered the strength to glare viciously at Weezing one last time.

The Terastallization light faded.

Its consciousness blurred.

Hisuian Arcanine collapsed.

"Weezing and Hisuian Arcanine have both lost the ability to battle."

The referee announced the result.

The stadium erupted.

"Finally beat that Hisuian Arcanine…"

"That thing was insane—trying to brute-force it would've been a team wipe."

"If not for Gengar and Weezing, taking it down would've been ridiculously hard."

"Let's hear it! What Iron Valiant and Mega Gengar couldn't do—Weezing did!"

"Anyone says Weezing's mid again and I'm throwing hands!"

The crowd buzzed loudly.

Dylan's tyrannical Hisuian Arcanine had finally fallen.

Which meant—

Everyone could now look forward to Dylan's next Pokémon.
